# Exposing Mystery Babylon

**[00:00:00]** Hey everybody, welcome back to the Dance of Life podcast.

**[00:00:11]** My name is Tudor Alexander and I'm your host as always.

**[00:00:13]** Thanks so much for being here with me.

**[00:00:15]** Jesus is the way, the truth, and the life, and you cannot get to the Father unless you

**[00:00:19]** go through him.

**[00:00:21]** Today we're going to be exposing, once and for all, Mystery Babylon, who has put herself

**[00:00:25]** between man and Jesus for hundreds of years, and a lot of the episodes we've been talking

**[00:00:32]** about in the last two months or so have been leading up to this point.

**[00:00:37]** So today I'm going to be talking about that in great detail.

**[00:00:40]** Make sure you have some popcorn and a notebook, but ultimately we're going to be getting into

**[00:00:46]** some really deep things.

**[00:00:47]** So make sure you subscribe because I don't know how these platforms will handle these

**[00:00:52]** types of contents over time.

**[00:00:53]** And the best way to stay in touch and to receive ongoing content is to subscribe on my website.

**[00:00:59]** And you can do so simply by going to danceoflife.com.

**[00:01:03]** There's a little subscription there.

**[00:01:04]** I probably email once a week, sometimes twice a week, nothing too crazy, but ultimately

**[00:01:09]** it's just the safest way to stay in touch.

**[00:01:12]** But today we are going to just jump right into it because we have a lot of material.

**[00:01:17]** So like I said, this will probably be a longer episode.

**[00:01:20]** You know, watch it in parts if you have to.

**[00:01:23]** Bookmark.

**[00:01:23]** Take notes.

**[00:01:25]** This is going to be a resource for you that by the end of this episode, you will absolutely

**[00:01:30]** for sure, without a doubt, 100% know who Mystery Babylon is.

**[00:01:34]** Now, if you're just joining us, you have some catching up to do.

**[00:01:37]** So make sure you do that.

**[00:01:39]** But ultimately, this episode can stand alone because the proof is in the pudding, as they

**[00:01:45]** say.
